# Geopolitical Briefing: State of the World as of June 1, 2026

Compiled for evaluation of mid-2024 predictions. All information sourced from publicly available reporting through June 1, 2026. Last updated: June 1, 2026.

> **Monthly scoring run — June 1, 2026.** This briefing was web-verified against current open-source reporting across every tracked theatre (US-Iran, Russia-Ukraine, DPRK, China-Taiwan, Venezuela/Cuba). No developments newer than the Day 95 cycle below were found. Scoring-relevant resolution this cycle: the **May 14-15 Beijing Trump-Xi summit** is now past — Trump publicly claimed Xi agreed to purchase US oil, LNG, energy and 200 Boeing 737 jets and to a 3-year "strategic stability" framework, but **no formal trade/energy deal was signed** and the two governments' readouts diverged (Al Jazeera, "Trump-Xi summit: China, US disagree on what they agreed on," May 15, 2026; S&P Global; CNBC, May 15, 2026). Ground-invasion rung **unchanged**: zero US ground troops in Iran at Day 95; the conflict is de-escalating toward a tentative, unsigned 60-day ceasefire MOU; Israel's Beaufort Castle capture (May 31) is an Israeli ground operation in Lebanon, not US ground troops in Iran.

---

### Trump Requests Edits to the MOU; No 'Final Determination' (May 31-June 1, 2026)
- After Friday May 29's roughly two-hour Situation Room meeting ended without a decision, **Trump requested amendments to the negotiated 60-day MOU rather than signing it.** A senior administration official told Axios he wants **"more specifics about how the U.S. gets the [enriched-uranium] material and the timing,"** and sought revisions to the **wording "around the reopening of the Strait of Hormuz."**
- The request launched **"another round of back-and-forth between the parties that could last several days"** — Iranian officials were given **roughly three days to respond,** with U.S. officials estimating completion at "a week," "less," or "more," hoping to resolve it **"at the turn of the week."**
- Trump said **"if you're going to be in a hurry, you're not going to make a good deal,"** and warned that if negotiations fail the U.S. will **"end it a different way,"** which would happen **"slowly but surely"** — an air/strike register, not a ground order. (Axios, "Trump requests edits to Iran deal his envoys negotiated," May 31, 2026; Fox News live blog, May 31, 2026.)

### MOU Content + Iranian Posture; Araghchi-Oman Call (May 31-June 1, 2026)
- The current draft includes Iran's commitment never to **"develop or in any way purchase a military weapon"** but, in its present form, **"no specific concessions beyond that pledge";** the 60-day window would put HEU disposition and enrichment limits first. **Iranian state media said the final text had not been approved.**
- FM **Abbas Araghchi** posted that he spoke with his **Omani counterpart** and **"expressed Iran's solidarity with Oman in face of any threat,"** as Oman — a longstanding back-channel mediator — was reported **caught between the U.S. and Iran over a possible Hormuz joint-management arrangement.** (Axios, May 31, 2026; Fox News live blog, May 31, 2026.)

### CONTESTED — Pezeshkian Resignation Report (May 31, 2026)
- **Iran International** (citing "a source familiar with the matter") and **The Jerusalem Post** reported that President **Masoud Pezeshkian submitted a letter of resignation** to the Office of the Supreme Leader (Mojtaba Khamenei), stating that the president and government had been **"excluded from major and vital decision-making processes,"** that **"hardline factions within the IRGC" had taken control,** and that he **"requested to step down immediately."**
- The **Iranian government denied** the report: **Tasnim News Agency** said Pezeshkian **"has not resigned and continues to carry out his duties,"** and the presidency's deputy communications head, **Seyed Mehdi Tabatabaei,** said the president **"will not retreat from serving the people."** The reported friction was over **"the way the war was managed and its destructive consequences."** No primary letter text has been published; open sources cannot confirm or rule out the resignation. (Iran International, "Iran's president offers resignation, citing total takeover by IRGC commanders," May 31, 2026; The Jerusalem Post, May 31, 2026; Tasnim News Agency / Daily Post Nigeria, May 31, 2026.)

### Lebanon — Israel Captures Beaufort Castle in Deepest Incursion in 26+ Years (May 31, 2026)
- The Israeli military **captured the Crusader-era, UNESCO-protected Beaufort Castle** near Nabatiyeh, roughly **14.5 km (9 miles) inside Lebanon and beyond the Litani River** — Israel's **deepest incursion into Lebanon in more than a quarter-century.**
- **IDF and Golani Brigade troops raised Israeli flags** over the castle after days of fierce fighting; **PM Netanyahu ordered forces to push deeper** into southern Lebanon; **France issued a strong condemnation.** The advance came **despite the nominal April 17 ceasefire;** Israel claims **~900 Hezbollah fighters killed** since the ceasefire began. This is an **Israeli ground operation in Lebanon, not US ground troops in Iran.** (CNN, "Israel seizes Crusader-era castle as Netanyahu orders forces deeper into Lebanon," May 31, 2026; The Washington Post; CBC; The Times of Israel; Euronews, May 31, 2026.)

### Blockade + Oil + Asian Energy Exposure (May 31-June 1, 2026)
- **CENTCOM's blockade tally rose to 118 commercial vessels redirected and 5 disabled** (120+ total affected since the blockade began); the **U.S. Navy maintains operational readiness** despite the ceasefire. The Strait of Hormuz remained **effectively closed.**
- **Oil markets were closed over the weekend;** Brent had **ended May near $92.56/bbl,** capping a **17-19% monthly decline — its worst month since 2020** — with analysts expecting prices to hold a **$90-100 band "for at least the next couple of months"** pending clarity on a lasting deal. Trump's request for edits (rather than a signature) keeps a residual risk premium in the price.
- In Asia, **LNG spot prices were up more than 140%;** **Japan and South Korea were releasing oil from their joint stockpile;** **Pakistan and Bangladesh remained the most exposed** (Qatar and the UAE supply ~99% of Pakistan's LNG, 72% of Bangladesh's, and 53% of India's), and India relies on the Strait for nearly half its crude and ~60% of its gas. (Fox News live blog, May 31, 2026; CNBC; Reuters/TradingEconomics; Zero Carbon Analytics; Gulf International Forum, May-June 2026.)

### Other Theatres — Russia-Ukraine / DPRK / Latin America (May 31-June 1, 2026)
- **Russia-Ukraine:** A **second direct round of talks remained expected ~June 2;** the **Pokrovsk sector** stayed the heaviest along the front, with Russia still unable to advance significantly west of Pokrovsk/Myrnohrad and both sides claiming frontline progress. (Wikipedia, "Pokrovsk offensive"; PBS, 2026.)
- **DPRK:** North Korea's **Choe Hyon-class destroyer** remained on track for a **mid-June commissioning** amid continued Kim Jong Un missile testing and a stated two-destroyers-per-year build goal. (SCMP; Newsweek; NBC News, 2026.)
- **Latin America:** **Maduro and Cilia Flores** were set to return to a Manhattan federal court on **June 30;** Cuba's grid crisis persisted amid the loss of Venezuelan oil (Cuba says 32 of its military/police were killed in the January U.S. operation). (PBS; Fox Business, 2026.)

### Carriers / USS Boxer ARG — Ground Rung Unchanged (May 31-June 1, 2026)
- **USS Boxer ARG** (LHD-6 + 11th MEU, ~5,000 Marines) still operating under **INDOPACOM;** CENTCOM arrival NOT yet formally confirmed. **USS Gerald R. Ford** home at Norfolk; **USS Abraham Lincoln + USS George H.W. Bush CSGs + USS Tripoli ARG** in the Arabian Sea — 20+ warships, 2 carriers, 1 ARG enforcing the blockade.
- **No new US ground-troop deployments, no new amphibious/third-ARG order, no BCT-scale National Guard activation, and no new Selective Service/draft action.** Trump's named deal-failure alternative ("end it a different way," "slowly but surely") is an air/strike register; the MOU's negotiated Hormuz reopening remains the explicit alternative to any Kharg/Hormuz ground seizure. **Israel's Beaufort Castle capture is an Israeli ground operation in Lebanon — not US ground troops in Iran — and does not move the US ground-invasion rung.** (USNI News Fleet & Marine Tracker; TWZ "Where Are The Carriers"; CNN, May 31, 2026.)

### Still No 'Final Determination'; Possible Sunday Announcement Floated (May 30-31, 2026)
- Following Friday May 29's roughly two-hour Situation Room meeting that ended with no decision, a **White House official said "President Trump will only make a deal that is good for America and satisfies his redlines."**
- **Trump and the mediators indicated the 60-day MOU could be announced Sunday May 31,** though it was **not finalized and "could still fall apart."** On Saturday May 31, **Lara Trump said she hoped the situation would "wrap up very quickly... maybe in the next couple hours,"** but no deal was announced.
- The MOU **remains double-pending** on both **Trump and Supreme Leader Mojtaba Khamenei** (out of public view since Feb 28). (CNBC, "Trump ends Iran meeting without announcing 'final determination' on deal," May 29, 2026; Fox News live, "Iran war news — May 30," May 30-31, 2026; PJ Media, "Will Donald Trump Sign the Iran Deal or Not?" May 30, 2026.)

### Iran Accuses Trump of Stalling With 'Excessive Demands' (May 30, 2026)
- NBC News headlined the day around Iranian pushback: **Mohsen Rezaei** (Expediency Council figure / former IRGC commander) accused Trump of **"betraying diplomacy"** through continued blockade enforcement, saying that **"by continuing the naval blockade and making excessive demands in negotiations, he has once again proven that he is not inclined toward negotiation and is pursuing other objectives."**
- The framing inverts Trump's own May 27 charge that Iran was stalling until the U.S. midterms. (NBC News, "Iran official says Trump is stalling talks with 'excessive demands' as wait for breakthrough continues," May 30, 2026.)

### Hegseth at Shangri-La Dialogue — US 'More Than Capable' of Resuming Strikes; Blockade 'Very Much Still in Place' (May 30, 2026)
- Speaking at the IISS **Shangri-La Dialogue in Singapore,** Secretary of War **Pete Hegseth** said the U.S. military is **"more than capable"** of renewed action against Iran if negotiations fail — **"Our stockpiles are more than suited for that both there and around the globe."**
- He described Trump as **"laser-focused"** on reaching a **"great deal,"** and said the **"ironclad" U.S. military blockade of Iranian ports** (in place since mid-April) is **"very much still in place"** and **"has put the real pressure on Iran."** The named deal-failure alternative remains an **air/strike package, not a ground order.** (RFE/RL, "US 'More Than Capable' Of Resuming Strikes Against Iran, Hegseth Says," May 30, 2026; Newsweek, "US Ready to Restart Attacks on Iran if Deal Falls Through — Hegseth," May 30, 2026; Fox News live, May 30, 2026.)

### CENTCOM Blockade Tally Rises; Oil Posts Worst Month Since 2020 (May 29-31, 2026)
- **CENTCOM reported roughly 115-116 commercial vessels redirected and five disabled** under the Iranian-port blockade — up from the May 23 milestone of 100+ redirected and four disabled — with the fifth disabled vessel identified as the **M/V Lian Star,** struck after **"more than 20 warnings."** The Strait of Hormuz remained **effectively closed.**
- **Oil markets were closed for the weekend.** Brent had **ended the week near $92/bbl,** capping a **~19% monthly decline — its worst month since the March 2020 COVID demand collapse** — and off about 20% from the 2026 peak on deal optimism. The **heads of four major global economic organizations warned oil reserves are being depleted "at a record pace"** amid the near-total Hormuz closure. Prices remain above the pre-war $60-70 (a residual risk premium) and well below the sustained $130+ ground-commitment level. (Fox News live, May 30, 2026; CNBC, "Oil drops 20% from 2026 peak," May 29, 2026; RFE/RL, May 30, 2026.)

### US-China — Hegseth Seeks 'Stable Equilibrium' at Shangri-La; Dong Jun Skips Forum (May 30, 2026)
- At the same forum, Hegseth said the U.S. seeks a **"stable equilibrium"** in the Asia-Pacific in which allies are more capable, **praised the Philippines, Australia, Indonesia, Malaysia and Singapore for "burden-sharing,"** urged **Vietnam and India** to improve military readiness, and **warned Beijing not to disrupt the status quo.**
- **Chinese Defence Minister Dong Jun skipped the Shangri-La Dialogue for a second consecutive year.** The forum was the first signal of U.S. Indo-Pacific policy since the **May 14 Trump-Xi Beijing summit,** where the two sides agreed to reset ties and manage disputes. (The Washington Post, "Pete Hegseth touts better China relations at Shangri-La Dialogues," May 30, 2026; SCMP; CNBC, "Hegseth praises Asian allies for 'burden-sharing,' calls out China's role in the region," May 30, 2026; France24, May 30, 2026.)

- **DPRK:** North Korea's destroyer **Choe Hyon** remained on track for a **mid-June commissioning** after Kim's May 7 inspection ordered the navy handover; no new ballistic-missile test was attributed to the May 30-31 cycle. (Al Jazeera; UPI; USNI News, May 2026.)
- **Russia-Ukraine:** A **second direct round of talks remained expected ~June 2;** the **Pokrovsk sector** stayed the heaviest along the front, with Russia unable to advance significantly west of Pokrovsk/Myrnohrad since December 2025; POW/humanitarian exchanges continue. (Wikipedia, "Pokrovsk offensive"; Russia Matters; Ukrinform, May 2026.)
- **Latin America:** The Maduro-Flores U.S. trial held its next New York court date for **June 30;** interim Venezuelan President **Delcy Rodriguez** continued offering to "collaborate" with Washington; **Cuba's grid crisis persisted.** (PBS; CBS News; Al Jazeera, 2026.)

- **USS Boxer ARG** (LHD-6 + 11th MEU, ~5,000 Marines) still operating under **INDOPACOM;** CENTCOM arrival NOT yet formally confirmed. **USS Gerald R. Ford** home at Norfolk; **USS Abraham Lincoln + USS George H.W. Bush CSGs + USS Tripoli ARG** in the Arabian Sea — 20+ warships, 2 carriers, 1 ARG enforcing the blockade.
- **No new US ground-troop deployments, no new amphibious/third-ARG order, no BCT-scale National Guard activation, and no new Selective Service/draft action.** Hegseth's Shangri-La messaging framed any deal-failure response as an air/strike package, not a ground order; the MOU's negotiated Hormuz reopening (no tolls + Iranian mine-clearing + proportional blockade lift) remains the explicit alternative to any Kharg/Hormuz ground seizure. ### Situation Room Meeting Ends Without a Decision (May 29, 2026)
- President Trump **ended a roughly two-hour meeting in the White House Situation Room without announcing his "final determination"** on whether to approve a deal to pause the three-month-old Iran war, despite saying earlier in the day that he would decide during that meeting.
- A **senior administration official told The New York Times** that an agreement was **"still close" but required further debate over issues including the unfreezing of Iranian funds.**
- The deal remains **double-pending:** both **President Trump and Supreme Leader Mojtaba Khamenei** must still give final approval, and there has been no definitive word that the Supreme Leader — **out of public view since his father's Feb 28 death,** issuing only written statements — has signed off. (CNN, "Live updates: Trump concludes Situation Room meeting on Iran without announcing a decision," May 29, 2026; CNBC, "Trump ends Iran meeting without announcing 'final determination' on deal," May 29, 2026; Axios, "Trump meets team to decide on Iran deal," May 29, 2026; The Times of Israel, "Trump meeting to make 'final determination' on Iran deal said to end with no decision," May 29, 2026; Euronews, "Iran-US deal nears finish line — but Trump and Khamenei must say yes," May 29, 2026; Bloomberg, "US, Iran Reach Deal on Extended Ceasefire Pending Trump Approval," May 29, 2026.)

### Trump Restates Demands on Truth Social; Iran Pushes Back (May 29, 2026)
- Trump posted that **Iran "must agree" that it will never have a nuclear weapon or bomb,** and that the **Strait of Hormuz "must be immediately open, no tolls, for unrestricted shipping traffic in both directions,"** with Iran also agreeing to **remove any remaining mines** in the strait.
- Iran's **Fars news agency** said the post **"raised issues that contradict the provisions of the agreement's text."** The IRGC-linked **Tasnim agency** said the **"final text of the agreement has not yet been completed or approved,"** while describing the MOU as **"in the final stages of approval in Iran"** with **"no final decision yet."** Iran continues to seek control over Strait of Hormuz management and to oppose removing its enriched uranium from the country. (CNBC; Euronews; The Jerusalem Post, "US-Iran ceasefire deal agreed upon, hangs on Trump, Khamenei approval," May 29, 2026.)

### Vance: Signing 'TBD'; 'A Couple of Language Points' (May 29, 2026)
- Vice President **JD Vance** said it was **"TBD"** whether President Trump would sign the tentative memorandum of understanding and that the two countries were still negotiating over **"a couple of language points,"** characterizing the talks as making **"good progress."** (CNN; The Times of Israel, "White House: US, Iran negotiators have agreed to MoU, but Trump's approval still needed," May 29, 2026.)

### Oil — Worst Month Since 2020: Brent ~$92.05 (-1.77%) / WTI ~$87.36 (-1.73%) (May 29, 2026)
- **WTI lost 1.73% to close at ~$87.36/bbl** while **Brent fell 1.77% to settle at ~$92.05/bbl** on May 29.
- **Brent fell just over 19% in May — its worst month since March 2020,** when the COVID-19 pandemic shut down economies — and is **down roughly 20% from the 2026 peak,** as markets priced optimism over a U.S.-Iran ceasefire and a potential Hormuz reopening.
- Prices remain **above the pre-war $60-70** (a residual risk premium) and **well below the sustained $130+** that ground-commitment pricing would require; structural floor (Hormuz closure + US blockade) intact. (CNBC, "Oil drops 20% from 2026 peak on optimism over U.S.-Iran ceasefire talks," May 29, 2026; CNBC, "Brent oil price posts biggest monthly loss in six years as market counts on a U.S.-Iran deal," May 29, 2026.)

### Air/Maritime Skirmishing Continues — Drone Shoot-Downs + Bandar Abbas Strike; Disputed Aircraft-Downing Claim (May 28-29, 2026)
- **US forces shot down Iranian one-way-attack drones** that posed a threat in and near the Strait of Hormuz and **struck an Iranian ground-control station at Bandar Abbas** as a further drone prepared for launch, preventing the launch.
- **Iranian state TV claimed early Friday — citing Jam (Bushehr) governor Masoud Tangestani — that a US aircraft was destroyed near Bushehr; CENTCOM denied the claim:** "No U.S. aircraft were shot down. All U.S. air assets are accounted for."
- Reported strike targets in the period included **Qeshm and Abu Musa near the Strait, Bandar Abbas, a refinery on Lavan Island, and the Asaluyeh petrochemical complex.** The exchange stayed in the **air/maritime register,** with both sides preserving the ceasefire framing. (Haaretz live, "CENTCOM denies Iranian claim that U.S. aircraft was shot down near Bushehr," May 29, 2026; OANN, "CENTCOM denies Iranian claim that it shot down a U.S. aircraft," May 29, 2026; The Jerusalem Post, "Bushehr governor claims Iran shot down US aircraft, US denies," May 29, 2026; NaturalNews; CENTCOM, May 28-29, 2026.)

### Russia-Ukraine — 189 Combat Clashes May 28; Possible Turning Point; Second Round ~June 2 (May 28, 2026)
- Ukraine's General Staff reported **189 combat engagements on May 28,** with the most active Russian assault attempts on the **Pokrovsk and Huliaipole sectors;** Zelensky-announced long-range operations continued.
- The **Soufan Center (May 28)** reiterated that Russia's **slowing advances and Ukraine's reestablished drone advantage** may indicate a turning point, while cautioning Ukraine could struggle without sustained US-European support. A **second direct round of talks is still expected ~June 2;** the 1,000-for-1,000 POW exchange continues. (Ukrinform; Ukrainska Pravda war updates, May 28, 2026; The Soufan Center, "IntelBrief," May 28, 2026; Wikipedia, "Timeline of the Russo-Ukrainian war (1 January 2026 – present).")

### East Asia — DPRK Destroyer Missile Tests; Kim Backs China (May 2026)
- North Korea's **Kim Jong Un supervised further missile tests from his new 5,000-ton destroyer Choe Hyon** over the weekend, with state media reporting the launch of **two strategic cruise missiles** (flying more than two hours) and **three anti-ship missiles** before they struck their targets — following five **Hwasong-11Ra** ballistic-missile launches with cluster-bomb/fragmentation-mine warheads earlier in May.
- In a meeting with Chinese FM **Wang Yi,** Kim backed Beijing's **"multipolar world"** push and the **one-China principle** on Taiwan. (NBC News, "North Korea's Kim Jong Un supervises missile tests from his naval destroyer," May 2026.)

### Lebanon / Latin America — Status (May 29-30, 2026)
- **Lebanon:** Health Ministry running tally holds at **≥3,185 killed / 9,633 wounded** since the Iran war began; the **45-day Israel-Lebanon ceasefire extension (May 15 start) holds structurally** with isolated strikes and Hezbollah-IDF skirmishes. Lebanon coverage remains a publicly named US-Iran MOU sticking point — Iran's FM insists any deal end the war on all fronts including Lebanon (Wikipedia, "2026 Lebanon war"; Al Jazeera; CFR).

- **USS Boxer ARG** (LHD-4 + 11th MEU, ~5,000 Marines) still operating under **INDOPACOM** per the TWZ/USNI carrier tracker; CENTCOM arrival NOT yet formally confirmed. **USS Gerald R. Ford** home at Norfolk; **USS Abraham Lincoln + USS George H.W. Bush CSGs + USS Tripoli ARG** in the Arabian Sea — 20+ warships, 2 carriers, 1 ARG enforcing the blockade.
- **No new US ground-troop deployments, no new amphibious/third-ARG order, no BCT-scale National Guard activation, and no new Selective Service/draft action.** The May 29 drone shoot-downs and Bandar Abbas ground-control-station strike were executed by in-theatre air/naval assets; the MOU's negotiated Hormuz reopening (no tolls + Iranian mine-clearing within 30 days + proportional blockade lift) remains the explicit alternative to any Kharg/Hormuz ground seizure. ### Axios Scoop: US + Iranian Negotiators Reach a Tentative 60-Day MOU; Trump Approval Pending (May 28, 2026)
- Axios reported that **US and Iranian negotiators have reached an agreement on a 60-day memorandum of understanding** to extend the ceasefire and launch negotiations on Iran's nuclear program, **but President Trump has yet to give his final approval, and Iran has not confirmed its acceptance.**
- A U.S. official said: **"The president relayed to the mediators that he wants a couple of days to think about it."**
- ABC News live coverage framed the state of play as **"negotiators believe they have a deal, but leaders haven't signed off"** — both **President Trump and Iran's Supreme Leader Mojtaba Khamenei** must still give their final stamps of approval. CBS reported negotiators have **"agreed to broad principles of agreement"** while Trump said **"time is on our side."**
- Axios called the signing of the MOU, if it happens, **the most significant diplomatic breakthrough since the war started,** while cautioning that a final agreement tackling Trump's nuclear demands would still require further intensive negotiations. (Axios, "Scoop: U.S. and Iran reach deal but need Trump's final approval, officials say," May 28, 2026; Al Jazeera, "US and Iran reach tentative deal for 60-day truce extension, officials say," May 28, 2026; ABC7/ABC News live updates; CBS News live updates; The Irish Times; Breitbart; May 28, 2026.)

### MOU Terms (Axios, May 28, 2026)
- **Strait of Hormuz shipping will be "unrestricted"** — meaning no tolls and no harassment — with **Iran required to remove all mines from the strait within 30 days.**
- The **U.S. would issue some sanctions waivers** to allow Iran to sell oil freely.
- The MOU would include an **Iranian commitment not to pursue a nuclear weapon,** and would state that the **first issues to be negotiated during the 60-day window are how to dispose of Iran's highly enriched uranium and how to address Iranian enrichment.**
- The **U.S. will commit to discuss sanctions relief and the release of frozen Iranian funds** as part of the negotiations. (Axios, "Scoop: U.S. and Iran reach deal but need Trump's final approval," May 28, 2026; Axios, "Exclusive: What's inside the Iran deal Trump is close to signing," May 24, 2026.)

### Carryover Contradiction: White House 'Complete Fabrication' / Trump 'No Relief for HEU' (May 27, 2026)
- The day before the Axios scoop, the **White House rapid-response team called an MOU draft published by Iranian state media a "complete fabrication" ("FACTS MATTER").** The Iranian draft claimed the U.S. would lift its naval blockade and withdraw troops from around Iran while Iran restored pre-war Hormuz shipping within a month.
- **Trump said Iran would NOT get sanctions relief in exchange for giving up highly enriched uranium,** but also told reporters at a Cabinet meeting that **"Iran is very much intent, they want very much to make a deal. So far they haven't gotten there ... we're not satisfied with it, but we will be."** (The Hill, "White House blasts draft Iran agreement as 'complete fabrication'"; The Week, "White House dismisses Iranian report on peace deal as 'complete fabrication'; Trump says no sanctions relief for Tehran"; U.S. News & World Report, "White House Says Iranian Media Report on Memorandum of Understanding Is False"; May 27, 2026.)

### Iran FM: Nuclear Not Part of Current Talks (May 28, 2026)
- Iran's Foreign Ministry stated that **nuclear issues are not part of the current negotiations,** which it framed as focused on **ending the war on all fronts (including Lebanon) and lifting sanctions on Tehran** — a framing in tension with the U.S. position that the 60-day window's first agenda items are HEU disposition and enrichment. (Wikipedia, "2025-2026 Iran-United States negotiations"; CBS News live updates; May 28, 2026.)

### Oil — Brent ~$96.29 (+2.1%) / WTI Back Above $90 (May 28, 2026)
- After the prior day's >5% crash, oil **partly rebounded** on the tentative-deal news: **Brent crude was at ~$96.29/bbl (+2.1%)** and **WTI futures returned above $90/bbl (+~2.4%).**
- CNBC ("Oil markets are betting on a swift end to the Iran war. Investors may regret it") noted low-to-mid-$90s prices still carry a **"clear risk premium"** unlikely to unwind to the **pre-war $60-70 level.** Both prints remain **well below the sustained $130+ that ground-commitment pricing would require;** structural floor intact (Hormuz closure + US blockade). (CNBC, May 28, 2026; Trading Economics.)

### Russia-Ukraine — 256 Combat Clashes May 27; Possible Turning Point; Second Round ~June 2 (May 27-28, 2026)
- Ukraine's General Staff reported **256 combat engagements on May 27,** with **nearly 50 concentrated in the Pokrovsk sector.** President **Zelensky announced new long-range operations** after meeting Commander-in-Chief **Syrskyi** and General Staff chief **Hnatov.**
- The **Soufan Center (May 28)** assessed that **Russia's slowing battlefield advances and Ukraine's reestablished drone advantage may indicate an important turning point,** while cautioning Ukraine **could struggle without sustained US-European support.** Russia continues to demand control of all of Donbas and denial of NATO membership; Ukraine rules out ceding territory. **Second direct round of talks expected ~June 2.** (Ukrinform; Ukrainska Pravda; The Soufan Center, "IntelBrief," May 28, 2026; Wikipedia, "Timeline of the Russo-Ukrainian war (1 January 2026 – present).")

### Trump Cabinet Meeting: Iran 'Negotiating on Fumes'; 'I Don't Care About the Midterms' (May 27, 2026)
- At a Cabinet meeting at the White House Wednesday May 27, **President Trump** asserted that Iran is **"negotiating on fumes"** and insisted **November's 2026 midterm elections won't factor in shaping his approach** to the nearly three-month-old conflict. Trump said Iran wanted to **"wait him out, counting on political pressure from the upcoming midterm elections,"** but said the **American people understand Iran can't have a nuclear weapon,** and declared **"I don't care about the midterms."**
- Under the potential deal, **Tehran would agree to give up its stockpile of highly enriched uranium — a key Trump demand — in return for sanctions relief.**
- **Another key issue unresolved is whether the ceasefire will also cover Israel's operations against Hezbollah, the Iranian-backed militant group in Lebanon. Iran has insisted that Lebanon must be covered by any ceasefire agreement negotiated with the United States.**
- The Washington Post: **"Trump gathers Cabinet as he looks to seal deal to end war that some backers worry will embolden Iran."** Pro-Israel critics — Sens. Cruz, Wicker, and former Sec State Pompeo — continued to attack the emerging 60-day MOU as **"a disastrous mistake" / "a disaster" / "not remotely America First."** (PBS NewsHour, "WATCH: In Cabinet meeting, Trump says Iran 'negotiating on fumes' and that midterms won't affect his war strategy," May 27, 2026; NPR, "Trump says Iran 'negotiating on fumes,' insists midterms won't impact his war strategy," May 27, 2026; CBS News, "Trump holds Cabinet meeting at the White House as negotiations continue to end Iran war," May 27, 2026; The Washington Post, "Trump gathers Cabinet as he looks to seal deal to end war that some backers worry will embolden Iran," May 27, 2026; CNN, "Live updates: Iran war; Trump says he won't rush Iran deal because 'I don't care about the midterms,'" May 27, 2026; Boston Globe live updates.)

### Rubio: 'Every Chance to Succeed'; Trump Has 'Other Options' If Talks Fail (May 27, 2026)
- **Secretary of State Marco Rubio** said talks with Iran have made some progress and the U.S. would give diplomacy **"every chance to succeed."** Rubio said President Trump has **"other options"** if talks don't work — **an apparent reference to renewed military strikes.** (CNBC, "Oil prices fall more than 5% after Rubio says U.S. will give Iran talks 'every chance to succeed,'" May 27, 2026; CBS News live updates.)

### Oil — WTI -5%+ to $88.68 / Brent -5%+ to $94.29 on Diplomatic Optimism (May 27, 2026)
- **West Texas Intermediate (WTI)** futures **tumbled more than 5% to close at ~$88.68/bbl.** International benchmark **Brent** oil also **lost more than 5% to settle at ~$94.29/bbl** on **Rubio's "every chance to succeed"** framing and Trump's Cabinet-meeting **"negotiating on fumes"** declaration.
- The move is squarely **two-state deal-close pricing ($90s-low $100s);** well below the sustained **$130+** that ground-commitment pricing would require. Structural floor intact (Hormuz closure + US blockade); NOT pricing ground commitment. (CNBC, "Oil prices fall more than 5% after Rubio says U.S. will give Iran talks 'every chance to succeed,'" May 27, 2026; CNBC, "Brent oil jumps more than 3% after Iran vows to retaliate for U.S. strikes," May 26 reference; Trading Economics; Fortune.)

### Iran 'No Deal Imminent' Line Holds — Baghaei + Araghchi + Shamkhani (May 26-27, 2026)
- Iran Foreign Ministry spokesman **Esmaeil Baghaei** restated **"to say this means an agreement is about to be signed — no one can make such a claim."** FM **Araghchi** (May 27) said he was **unsure whether a deal was imminent.**
- **Khamenei adviser Ali Shamkhani** dismissed Trump's desired control over the Iranian nuclear program as **"fantasy."** Iranian President **Masoud Pezeshkian** said Iran is **"ready to reassure the world that we are not seeking nuclear weapons"** but stressed negotiators **"will not compromise when it comes to our country's honor and dignity."** (NBC News, "Iran says no deal 'imminent' despite progress in talks with U.S.," May 26-27, 2026; Wikipedia, "2025-2026 Iran-United States negotiations"; Breitbart Europe, "Iranian President: Ready to Assure World Not Seeking Nuclear Weapons.")

### IRGC Claims Downing US MQ-9 Reaper Over Persian Gulf — UNCONFIRMED (May 26, 2026 — amplified May 27)
- The **IRGC** said its **air defense units intercepted and shot down a US MQ-9 Reaper drone over the Persian Gulf** while "defending Iran's territorial airspace"; said it also **fired shots at an RQ-4 Global Hawk surveillance drone and an F-35** supersonic stealth fighter, **"forcing them to flee"** Iranian airspace.
- The **US military has not commented** on the IRGC's claims. The IRGC repeated its **"beyond the region"** / **"right of reciprocal response"** framing. (PressTV, "IRGC downs US MQ-9 Reaper drone over Persian Gulf, warning against ceasefire violations," May 26, 2026; Iran International, "IRGC says it downed US MQ-9 drone over Persian Gulf," May 26, 2026; Tasnim News Agency, "IRGC Downs US MQ-9 Drone over Persian Gulf, Warns against Ceasefire Violations," May 26, 2026; GlobalSecurity.)

### Russia-Ukraine — 296 Combat Clashes May 27; Pokrovsk Front ~53 Attacks Repelled (May 27, 2026)
- Ukraine's General Staff: **296 combat clashes** between Ukraine's Defense Forces and Russian troops were recorded on **May 27,** with Russians conducting most assaults on the **Pokrovsk front,** where Ukrainian forces **repelled 53 attacks** near Nykanorivka, Kotlyne, Horikhove, Novooleksandrivka, Rodynske, Pokrovsk, Hryshyne, Udachne and toward Shevchenko, Bilytske, Serhiivka, Novopavlivka.
- Russian forces shelled the **Korabelnyi district of Kherson** using multiple launch rocket systems, with a **children's playground** where parents and children were spending time coming under fire; **eight people were injured in Odesa** after a drone struck a shopping mall on the afternoon of May 27.
- **President Zelensky** announced new long-range operations against Russia after a meeting with Commander-in-Chief **Syrskyi** and Chief of the General Staff **Hnatov;** the Unmanned Systems Forces carried out strikes against key Russian military targets in temporarily occupied Luhansk, Donetsk and Zaporizhzhia regions.
- The **second direct Russia-Ukraine round** of talks is still expected **~June 2;** the 1,000-for-1,000 POW exchange continues (US guarantor). (Ukrainska Pravda, "Battlefield sees almost 300 clashes over past 24 hours – Ukraine's General Staff," May 27, 2026; Ukrinform war update; Wikipedia, "Timeline of the Russo-Ukrainian war (1 January 2026 – present)"; Russia Matters "Russia-Ukraine War Report Card.")

### Lebanon — 45-Day Extended Ceasefire Holds Structurally; ≥3,185 Killed / 9,633 Wounded (May 27-28, 2026)
- **Lebanon Health Ministry** running tally holds at **≥3,185 killed / 9,633 wounded** by Israeli strikes in Lebanon since the Iran war began (no fresh aggregate released May 27-28). The Israel-Lebanon ceasefire (initial 10 days from Apr 16, three-week extension from Apr 26, **45-day extension from May 15**) holds in structure but UN reports prior-week attacks were the most intense since the truce started; isolated strikes and Hezbollah skirmishes continue.
- The **Lebanon-coverage** issue is now a public, named sticking point in the emerging US-Iran 60-day MOU. (Wikipedia "2026 Lebanon war"; Wikipedia "Timeline of the 2026 Lebanon war"; Wikipedia "2026 Israel-Lebanon ceasefire"; CFR "Israel-Lebanon Ceasefire Extended for Three Weeks"; UNIFIL releases.)

### Carriers / USS Boxer ARG — Ground Rung Unchanged (May 27-28, 2026)
- **USS Boxer ARG** (LHD-4 + Comstock + Portland + 11th MEU, ~5,000 Marines) **STILL operating under INDOPACOM** per the TWZ/USNI carrier tracker; CENTCOM arrival **not yet formally confirmed.**
- **USS Gerald R. Ford (CVN-78) home at Norfolk;** **USS Abraham Lincoln + USS George H.W. Bush CSGs + USS Tripoli ARG** in the Arabian Sea — 20+ warships, 2 carriers, 1 ARG enforcing the blockade. CENTCOM still reports **MORE THAN 15,000 US troops** committed to the Apr 13 naval blockade within the ~50,000+ CENTCOM baseline.
- **No new amphibious/Marine order; no flank-speed expedite, no third ARG; no BCT-scale National Guard activation; no Selective Service/draft language;** Murkowski draft AUMF would BAN ground troops. The US response register stays **AIR/MARITIME ('other options' = air-strike package), not a ground order.** (TWZ; USNI Fleet & Marine Tracker; Atlantic Council "Tracking US military assets in the Iran war.")

### Other Theatres (May 27-28, 2026)
- **Latin America:** **Cuba** — Havana blackout protests persist; energy crisis continues with diesel and fuel-oil stocks 'virtually run out.' **Venezuela** — Maduro and Cilia Flores' next NYC court date is **June 30;** acting President Delcy Rodriguez continues the Caracas-cooperation pattern; **Alex Saab** (deported May 17) faces Miami money-laundering charges and may testify against Maduro. (Wikipedia "2026 Cuban crisis"; Wikipedia "2026 United States intervention in Venezuela.")
- **East Asia / DPRK:** No new DPRK BM launch reported May 27-28; Kim continues to invoke the Iran war to justify the nuclear arsenal as 'irreversible'; **Choe Hyon** destroyer commissioning expected **mid-June** per prior KCNA reporting.
- **US-China:** No major US-China trade or Taiwan announcement May 27-28; **Section 301 tariff-restoration framework** remains under negotiation; Trump-Xi summit outcomes from May 14-15 (Hormuz 'demilitarized + no tolls'; Iran 'cannot have a nuclear weapon'; China 'no military equipment to Iran') hold rhetorically but Beijing-Washington divergence persists on Taiwan.

### CENTCOM 'Self-Defense Strikes' on Two IRGC Mine-Laying Boats + Bandar Abbas SAM Site (May 25-26, 2026)
- **US Central Command** announced **"self-defense strikes"** in southern Iran early Tuesday May 26: **two IRGC vessels destroyed in the Strait of Hormuz while reportedly laying mines,** and a **surface-to-air missile site near Bandar Abbas** hit after it was **"targeting US aircraft."** CENTCOM said the strikes were **"in direct response to 24 hours of missile, drone and small boat launches carried out by Iran's Islamic Revolutionary Guard Corps near the Strait of Hormuz"** and that it **"continues to defend its forces while using restraint during the ongoing ceasefire."**
- **Iranian state television** citing **Fars News Agency** reported **four people were killed** in Bandar Abbas; total casualties remained unclear. (CENTCOM; CBS News live updates, "U.S. conducts 'self-defense' strikes, CENTCOM says, insists ceasefire still in place amid negotiations," May 26, 2026; CNBC, "U.S. conducts 'self-defense strikes' in Iran as Trump pushes for peace deal," May 26, 2026; The Aviationist, "U.S. Launches 'Defensive Strikes' in Iran Targeting Mine-Laying Boats and SAM Sites," May 26; Xinhua, May 26; IBTimes UK, May 26; Townhall, May 25.)

### Iran 'Flagrant Violation' / 'Far More Severe' Retaliation Vow; Khamenei: 'US Bases No Longer Safe' (May 25-26, 2026)
- The **IRGC** called the strikes a **"clear / flagrant violation"** of the April 7-8 ceasefire and said it considers **"the right to reciprocal response to be legitimate and certain."** Iranian army spokesperson **Brig. Gen. Abolfazl Shekarchi** told **Fars** that **"any new attack on Iran will generate a 'far more severe' response 'beyond the region'"** and **"heavier than before."**
- **Supreme Leader Mojtaba Khamenei** warned that **US bases in the region are "no longer safe"** after the strikes. (NBC News, "Iran accuses U.S. of violating ceasefire and threatens retaliation after new strikes," May 25-26, 2026; CNN, "Live updates: Iran war news; Iran's IRGC threatens to retaliate after US strikes on launch sites and boats," May 25-26; Al Jazeera, "US strikes Iran again: What we know, and is the ceasefire over?," May 26; Euronews, "Gulf braces for Iran's response as US strikes shake peace talks," May 26.)

### Trump: Strikes 'Just A Love Tap'; Ceasefire Intact (May 26, 2026)
- President **Trump** downplayed the strikes in remarks to ABC News reporters as **"just a love tap"** and insisted the ceasefire with Iran remained in effect. **CNN Politics** characterized the pattern as **"When Iran thumbs its nose at the ceasefire, the Trump administration shrugs."** Trump's posture is consistent with the pre-strike "time is on our side" / "not to rush" framing of May 24-25. (CNN Politics, "When Iran thumbs its nose at the ceasefire, the Trump administration shrugs," May 26, 2026; ABC News live updates.)

### Doha Track: Iranian Delegation (Araghchi + Qalibaf + Hemmati) Meets Qatari Mediators (May 25-27, 2026)
- An **Iranian delegation** arrived in **Doha on Monday May 25** for talks with **Qatari mediators** on the proposed US-Iran agreement to end the war: **Foreign Minister Abbas Araghchi,** **Parliament Speaker Mohammad Bagher Qalibaf** (the chief negotiator in the talks with the US), and **Central Bank Governor Abdolnaser Hemmati** met Qatari diplomats on **Sheikh Tamim bin Hamad Al Thani's instructions;** the discussions were described as **"generally positive"** and chiefly addressed Iran's **~$24 billion in frozen funds** and the **disposition of Iran's nuclear stockpile.**
- Washington and Tehran have observed the ceasefire since **April 8;** both sides are talking of a **"memorandum of understanding"** that will set out a roadmap for resolving all outstanding issues, although a deal is still a **"work in progress."** (The National (UAE), "Iran war latest: Iran's Ghalibaf and Araghchi in Doha for talks on agreement with US," May 25, 2026; PressTV, "Iran's top negotiator Qalibaf, FM Araghchi in Qatar amid talks to end war," May 25; Al Jazeera, "Iranian delegation in Qatar as talks on US ceasefire extend; Rubio says US will find another way if Iran talks fail," May 25.)

### Deal '95% There' / 'Close to Finalizing' / 'Strong Inspections' vs 'Not Imminent' / 'Fantasy' (May 26-27, 2026)
- **Fox News,** citing **senior US officials,** reported Monday May 26 that the Iran deal was **"95 percent there."** On Tuesday May 27 **President Trump** said both sides were **"close to finalizing an agreement"** involving **"strong inspections"** of Iran's nuclear program. US officials said **Supreme Leader Mojtaba Khamenei** had **"endorsed the broad template of the deal,"** though formal Iranian approval could take **"several additional days."**
- **Iran FM Araghchi** (May 27) said he was **unsure whether a deal was imminent;** FM spokesman **Esmaeil Baghaei** said **"to say this means an agreement is about to be signed — no one can make such a claim."** **Khamenei adviser Ali Shamkhani** dismissed Trump's desired control over the Iranian nuclear program as **"fantasy."**
- Iranian President **Masoud Pezeshkian** said Iran is **"ready to reassure the world that we are not seeking nuclear weapons"** but that negotiators **"will not compromise when it comes to our country's honor and dignity."** Iran's standing precondition for a full deal remains complete lifting of the US naval blockade. (Fox News; Wikipedia "2025-2026 Iran-United States negotiations," May 26-27 entries; NBC News, "Iran says no deal 'imminent' despite progress in talks with U.S."; Breitbart Europe, "Iranian President: Ready to Assure World Not Seeking Nuclear Weapons.")

### Austria Intelligence (DSN, May 26): Iran Pursuing Advanced Nuclear Weapons Program; Iran Rejects (May 26, 2026)
- Austria's **domestic intelligence service (DSN)** reported on May 26 that **Iran is pursuing an advanced nuclear weapons program** and is **developing ballistic missiles capable of carrying nuclear warheads over long distances.** Iran rejected the assessment as **"false and baseless"** and an attempt to create **"media hype"** against Iran. (Wikipedia "2025-2026 Iran-United States negotiations," May 26 entry.)

### Iran Internet Blackout Partially Lifted After 87 Days (May 26-27, 2026)
- Iran's **nationwide internet blackout,** in place since the early days of the war, was **partially lifted after 87 days** — a soft normalization signal coinciding with the Doha talks. (ABC News, "Iran live updates: Iran's internet blackout partially lifted after 87 days," May 26-27, 2026; ABC7 New York; Wikipedia "2026 Iran war.")

### Oil and Blockade Metrics (May 26-27, 2026)
- **Brent crude futures** gained **more than 3% to close ~$99.58/bbl Tuesday May 26** on Iran's retaliation threats; **West Texas Intermediate** fell **~3% to settle ~$93.89/bbl** — the divergence reflects mixed signals (Hormuz risk premium upward; deal-close + demand softness downward). Both prints remain **well below the sustained $130+ that ground-commitment pricing would require.** (CNBC, "Brent oil jumps more than 3% after Iran vows to retaliate for U.S. strikes," May 26, 2026.)
- The **US naval blockade remains in force.** CENTCOM's May 23 milestone stands: **more than 100 commercial vessels redirected** since Apr 13 (4 disabled, 26 humanitarian-aid vessels passed), **>15,000 US troops** enforcing; **>1,500 vessels / ~22,500 mariners** still trapped — maritime-blockade enforcement, not ground-assault staging.

- **USS Boxer ARG** (LHD-4 + Comstock + Portland + 11th MEU, ~5,000 Marines) **STILL operating under INDOPACOM** per the TWZ/USNI carrier tracker; CENTCOM arrival **not yet formally confirmed.**
- **USS Gerald R. Ford (CVN-78) home at Norfolk;** **USS Abraham Lincoln + USS George H.W. Bush CSGs + USS Tripoli ARG** in the Arabian Sea.
- The first kinetic US-Iran exchange of the Project Freedom pause was executed by **in-theatre carrier/destroyer assets** — **no new amphibious/Marine order; no flank-speed expedite, no third ARG; no BCT-scale National Guard activation; no Selective Service/draft language;** **Murkowski draft AUMF would BAN ground troops.** The US response register stays **AIR/MARITIME 'self-defense,' not a ground order.** (TWZ; USNI Fleet & Marine Tracker; Atlantic Council "Tracking US military assets in the Iran war.")

- **Lebanon Health Ministry:** at least **3,185 killed and 9,633 wounded** by Israeli strikes in Lebanon since the Iran war began (up from ≥3,020 / 9,273 on May 18). On the Israeli side, **2 civilians and 16 IDF soldiers** have been killed in Lebanon-connected operations through late April.
- The **Israel-Lebanon ceasefire** (initial 10 days from Apr 16, three-week extension from Apr 26) holds in structure but **UN reports this week's attacks were the most intense since the truce started.** (Wikipedia "2026 Lebanon war"; Wikipedia "Timeline of the 2026 Lebanon war"; Wikipedia "2026 Israel-Lebanon ceasefire"; CFR "Israel-Lebanon Ceasefire Extended for Three Weeks"; UNIFIL releases.)

### Trump (May 25): Ties Any Final Iran Deal to a Widened Abraham Accords — 'Mandatorily Requesting' All Countries Sign
- On Truth Social (Monday May 25), **President Trump** urged several majority-Muslim countries to join the **Abraham Accords** as part of the settlement he is seeking with Iran: **"I am mandatorily requesting that all Countries immediately sign the Abraham Accords, and that, if Iran signs its Agreement with me, as President of the United States of America, it would be an Honor to have them also be part of this unparalleled World Coalition."** He named **Egypt, Jordan, Pakistan, Qatar and Saudi Arabia** as nations that should establish full diplomatic relations with Israel, adding it **"should be mandatory that all of these Countries, at a minimum, simultaneously, sign onto the Abraham Accords."**
- Trump said the Iran negotiations were **"proceeding nicely"** but **gave no indication a deal was imminent;** he and administration officials continued to temper expectations while calling for more Middle East countries to normalize ties with Israel as part of any deal. (CNBC, "Trump links Abraham Accords to Iran deal," May 25; NPR, "Trump says more countries should normalize ties with Israel in any Iran deal," May 25; The Washington Post, "Trump seeks to widen Abraham Accords as new Iran deal faces sharp criticism," May 25.)

### Emerging 60-Day MOU Draws Sharp Pro-Israel Criticism (Cruz, Wicker, Pompeo); Graham Reverses to 'Simply Brilliant' (May 25, 2026)
- **Sen. Ted Cruz** warned an Iranian regime **"now receiving billions of dollars"** while able to **"enrich uranium & develop nuclear weapons"** would be **"a disastrous mistake."** **Sen. Roger Wicker** called the rumored 60-day ceasefire **"a disaster."** Former Secretary of State **Mike Pompeo** likened the deal to the 2015 nuclear agreement and said the approach was **"not remotely America First."**
- **Sen. Lindsey Graham,** who had called reopening the Strait of Hormuz to end the war a **"nightmare"** for Israel, **reversed** after Trump's normalization proposal, calling it **"simply brilliant";** commentator **Mark Levin,** initially critical of the Iran deal, praised the normalization as **"a truly massive accomplishment!"** (Al Jazeera, "Trump dangles normalisation amid pro-Israel criticism of possible Iran deal," May 25; The Washington Post, May 25.)

### Rubio: Deal 'Could Materialize Today' / 'Alternatives' if Diplomacy Fails; Iran FM: 'Large Portion of the Issues' Concluded but 'Not Imminent' (May 25, 2026)
- **Secretary of State Rubio** said a deal **"could materialize today,"** expressing confidence in reopening the Strait of Hormuz while keeping open **"alternatives"** if diplomacy fails.
- **Iran's foreign-ministry spokesman** said **"it is correct to say that we have reached a conclusion on a large portion of the issues,"** but cautioned that **"an agreement is not imminent,"** accusing Washington of **shifting positions.** Reported sticking points remained the **status/management of the Strait of Hormuz** (Iran insists it "remain under Iranian management" vs the US assertion of a full reopening), the **timing of the release of Iran's ~$25B frozen assets** (Iran wants them released before any nuclear agreement), and **whether the deal ends the Israel-Lebanon war** (Netanyahu wants to retain the right to continue it). (CBS News live updates, "Iran-U.S. negotiators have agreed to broad principles of agreement, official says; Trump says time is on our side," May 25; Yahoo News, "Iran to surrender uranium and open Hormuz in US deal," May 24-25.)

### Pakistan Army Chief Asim Munir + PM Meet Chinese Leaders in Beijing on the Iran File (May 25, 2026)
- **Pakistan's army chief Field Marshal Asim Munir,** alongside Pakistan's **prime minister,** met **Chinese leaders in Beijing** on the Iran file — drawing **China further into the mediation effort** that had run through Pakistan and Qatar. (CBS News live updates, May 25.)

## 1-PRIOR (MAY 24-25). US-Iran Military Actions — MAY 24-25 (DAY 88): DECISION SUNDAY RESOLVES TOWARD A DELIBERATE NON-RUSH — ONE DAY AFTER THE 'LARGELY NEGOTIATED' ANNOUNCEMENT, TRUMP (TRUTH SOCIAL, SUN MAY 24) TELLS HIS REPRESENTATIVES 'NOT TO RUSH INTO A DEAL' WITH IRAN: 'BOTH SIDES MUST TAKE THEIR TIME AND GET IT RIGHT' — DECLARES THE US NAVAL BLOCKADE WILL 'REMAIN IN FULL FORCE AND EFFECT UNTIL AN AGREEMENT IS REACHED, CERTIFIED, AND SIGNED' — FRAMES TALKS AS 'ORDERLY AND CONSTRUCTIVE' / 'TIME IS ON OUR SIDE' — WHITE HOUSE: NO DEAL FINALIZED SUNDAY; KHAMENEI APPROVAL COULD TAKE 'SEVERAL ADDITIONAL DAYS' BUT 'SIGNED WITHIN DAYS'; SENIOR US OFFICIAL: 'WE ARE IN A VERY GOOD PLACE — BUT THERE ARE WAYS IN WHICH THE DEAL CAN BE UNDERMINED' — AXIOS 'WHAT'S INSIDE': 60-DAY MOU (RENEWABLE); HORMUZ REOPENS WITHOUT TOLLS + IRAN CLEARS ITS DEPLOYED MINES; US LIFTS THE PORT BLOCKADE + SANCTIONS WAIVERS ('RELIEF FOR PERFORMANCE'); IRAN PLEDGES NEVER TO PURSUE A NUCLEAR WEAPON + 'VERBAL COMMITMENTS' VIA MEDIATORS ON ENRICHMENT-SUSPENSION/HEU-REMOVAL TALKS; ASSETS/SANCTIONS RELIEF ONLY AFTER FINAL-DEAL IMPLEMENTATION; US FORCES REMAIN 60 DAYS, WITHDRAW ONLY ON A FINAL DEAL; LEBANON WAR MUST END — IRAN TASNIM: 'ONE OR TWO CLAUSES' PERSIST; NO FIRST-STEP ASSET RELEASE = 'NO AGREEMENT'; IRGC-LINKED OUTLETS: HORMUZ TO 'REMAIN UNDER IRANIAN SUPERVISION' — OIL: MARKETS CLOSED FOR THE HOLIDAY WEEKEND (US MEMORIAL DAY + UK BANK HOLIDAY MON MAY 25); BRENT ~$105 LAST (FRI) PRINT, -~6% ON WEEK — USS BOXER ARG STILL UNDER INDOPACOM; USS GERALD R. FORD HOME AT NORFOLK; LINCOLN + BUSH CSGs IN ARABIAN SEA — NOTE: THE DECISION-SUNDAY SLOW-WALK (BLOCKADE HELD 'IN FULL FORCE' + STRIKE BACKSTOP INTACT) REMAINS AIR/STRIKE-PACKAGE REGISTER, NOT A GROUND-INVASION ORDER (NO NEW TROOP MOVEMENTS, NO THIRD ARG, NO DRAFT); THE EMERGING DEAL'S 'US-FORCES-WITHDRAW-ON-FINAL-DEAL' CLAUSE IS DEESCALATORY AT THE GROUND RUNG (CONTESTED-UNRESOLVED — open-questions: nyt-intense-preparations-iran-resumption-may-2026)

### Trump (May 24): 'Not to Rush Into a Deal'; Blockade to 'Remain in Full Force... Until... Signed'
- One day after announcing a deal was **"largely negotiated,"** **President Trump wrote on Truth Social (Sunday May 24)** that he had told his representatives **"not to rush into a deal"** with Iran, that **"both sides must take their time and get it right,"** and that the **US naval blockade would "remain in full force and effect until an agreement is reached, certified, and signed."** He framed the talks as **"orderly and constructive,"** said the US-Iran relationship had become **"more professional and productive,"** and that **"time is on our side."** (Reuters via The Detroit News / Honolulu Star-Advertiser, "Trump says no rush for Iran deal, US blockade stays," May 24; Washington Times, "One day after Iran deal breakthrough, Trump taps brakes"; OANN.)

### White House: No Deal Finalized Sunday; 'Signed Within Days' Pending Khamenei Approval (May 24, 2026)
- **White House officials** said they **did not expect a deal to be finalized Sunday;** approval by Iran's Supreme Leader **Mojtaba Khamenei** could take **"several additional days,"** though officials remained **optimistic it would be "signed within days."** A **senior US official** told CNN: **"We are in a very good place — but there are ways in which the deal can be undermined."** (Axios, "Trump officials: Iran deal to end war may take days," May 24; CNN live updates, May 24.)

### Axios 'What's Inside the Deal' — 60-Day MOU; Hormuz Reopening + Iranian Mine-Clearing; US Forces Withdraw Only on a Final Deal (May 24, 2026)
- The emerging deal is a **60-day memorandum of understanding (renewable by mutual consent)** under which **the Strait of Hormuz reopens without tolls and Iran clears the mines it deployed;** the **US lifts its port blockade and issues sanctions waivers** to allow Iran to **sell oil** on a **"relief for performance"** basis (faster mine-clearance triggers a faster blockade lift).
- **Nuclear:** Iran **pledges never to pursue a nuclear weapon** and has given **"verbal commitments"** through mediators on **negotiations to suspend enrichment and remove its HEU.** **Assets/sanctions:** frozen assets are **unfrozen and sanctions lifted only after final-deal implementation.** **Forces:** **US military forces remain in the region during the 60-day period and withdraw only upon a final deal.** **Lebanon:** the deal **stipulates the Lebanon war must end** (Netanyahu expressed concerns; a US official framed reciprocal restraint as **"if Hezbollah behaves, Israel will behave"**). (Axios, "Exclusive: What's inside the Iran deal Trump is close to signing," May 24; CNN, "What's in the proposed deal that could end the US-Iran conflict?," May 24; Israel Hayom, "Emerging Iran deal starts countdown to the next war," May 24.)

### Iran Pushback — Tasnim: 'One or Two Clauses' Persist; No First-Step Asset Release = 'No Agreement'; Hormuz 'Under Iranian Supervision' (May 24, 2026)
- Iran's semi-official **Tasnim news agency** said Sunday that differences over **"one or two clauses"** of the MOU **persist,** and that **"without the release of a specific portion of Iran's blocked assets in this very first step — along with a clear mechanism for the guaranteed, continued release of all blocked assets — there will be no agreement."** Multiple **Iranian outlets, some close to the IRGC,** reported the strait would **"remain under Iranian supervision";** a senior US official countered that the **unfreezing of assets will occur only once Hormuz has reopened** — leaving the **Hormuz-supervision and asset-sequencing clauses** the two live sticking points. (CNN, "What's in the proposed deal...," May 24; Reuters via Detroit News/Star-Advertiser, May 24.)

### Oil and Blockade Metrics (May 24-25, 2026)
- **Oil markets were closed for the holiday weekend** (US Memorial Day + UK spring bank holiday both fall **Monday May 25**); no new print. **Brent's last print was ~$105/bbl** at Friday's close, **down ~6% on the week** and **~50% above pre-war levels;** WTI near the high-$90s. Trump's "not to rush" / blockade-"in full force" posture + the emerging 60-day MOU keep prices in **deal-close range, well below the sustained $130+ that ground-commitment pricing would require.**
- **The US naval blockade is held "in full force and effect until an agreement is reached, certified, and signed."** CENTCOM's May 23 milestone stands: **more than 100 commercial vessels redirected** since Apr 13 (4 disabled, 26 humanitarian-aid vessels passed), **>15,000 US troops** enforcing; **>1,500 vessels / ~22,500 mariners** still trapped — maritime-blockade enforcement, not ground-assault staging.

### Carriers / USS Boxer ARG — Ground-Invasion Indicators (May 24-25, 2026)
- **USS Boxer ARG** (LHD-4 + Comstock + Portland + 11th MEU, ~5,000 Marines) **STILL operating under INDOPACOM in the Indian Ocean** per the TWZ/USNI carrier tracker; CENTCOM arrival **not yet formally confirmed.**
- **USS Gerald R. Ford (CVN-78) home at Norfolk;** **USS Abraham Lincoln + USS George H.W. Bush CSGs** in the Arabian Sea (20+ warships enforcing the blockade).
- **No new troop movements; no third ARG; no BCT-scale National Guard activation; no Selective Service/draft language; Murkowski draft AUMF would BAN ground troops.** The named deal-failure alternative remains an **air strike** ("hit harder"), not a ground order, and the emerging deal's **"US-forces-withdraw-on-final-deal"** clause is **structurally deescalatory at the ground rung.** (TWZ; USNI Fleet & Marine Tracker; Atlantic Council "Tracking US military assets in the Iran war.")

### Other Theatres (May 24-25, 2026)
- **Russia-Ukraine:** the **1,000-for-1,000 POW exchange** continues with the **US acting as guarantor;** a **second round of direct talks is expected ~June 2;** Putin still declines to meet Zelensky outside a third country; Russia controls just under one-fifth of Ukraine. (Kyiv Independent; CBS News; Al Jazeera.)
- **Venezuela / Cuba:** Maduro and Flores are next due in a New York federal court **June 30;** Maduro ally **Alex Saab** has been **deported to the US to testify;** acting President Delcy Rodriguez consolidates; **Cuba's Havana blackout protests persist.** (Wikipedia "Prosecution of Nicolas Maduro and Cilia Flores" / "2026 United States intervention in Venezuela.")
- **North Korea:** **no new ballistic-missile test;** the **Choe Hyon destroyer handover is scheduled mid-June;** the "irreversible nuclear" framing continues. (NPR; Bloomberg.)
- **US-China:** the post-summit "stabilization" holds after the **May 14-15 Beijing summit** (Xi invited to the White House Sept 24; Xi's Taiwan warnings; trade truce moving toward a formal agreement); no new May 24-25 development. (CNBC; CBS News.)
- **Hormuz-affected economies:** Japanese refiners have asked Tokyo to release stockpiled oil; India cut excise duties amid LPG queues; Pakistan — among the worst-hit — continues rerouting crude via Saudi Arabia's Red Sea port of Yanbu; US gasoline elevated toward $5/gal. (Wikipedia "2026 Iran war fuel crisis"; WEF; IEA.)

## 1-PRIOR (MAY 23-24). US-Iran Military Actions — MAY 23-24 (DAY 87): DECISION WEEKEND — TRUMP SAYS A DEAL IS 'LARGELY NEGOTIATED' AND A FRAMEWORK WILL BE 'ANNOUNCED SHORTLY' (INCL. REOPENING THE STRAIT OF HORMUZ) WHILE FRAMING THE ODDS A 'SOLID 50/50' BETWEEN A 'GOOD' DEAL AND HITTING IRAN 'HARDER THAN THEY HAVE EVER BEEN HIT' — MEDIATORS CONVERGE ON A 60-DAY CEASEFIRE-EXTENSION FRAMEWORK WHOSE FIRST PHASE IS A 14-CLAUSE MEMORANDUM OF UNDERSTANDING (IRAN FM SPOX BAQAEI), NUCLEAR FILE DEFERRED TO A SEPARATE 30-60-DAY TRACK; GRADUAL HORMUZ REOPENING WITHOUT TOLLS; IRAN TO 'DISCUSS' DILUTING/TRANSFERRING ITS ~440 KG HEU; LIMITED SANCTIONS RELIEF + PHASED ASSET RELEASE — TRUMP CONVENES A SATURDAY MEETING WITH WITKOFF + KUSHNER (VANCE EXPECTED) + A CONFERENCE CALL WITH MEDIATOR/GULF LEADERS (EGYPT, PAKISTAN, TURKEY), SAYS HE WILL DECIDE BY SUNDAY — RUBIO (MAY 23): 'SOME PROGRESS,' 'WE MAY HAVE SOMETHING TO SAY' — PAKISTAN FIELD MARSHAL ASIM MUNIR MEETS PRESIDENT PEZESHKIAN; TALKS 'HIGHLY PRODUCTIVE' / 'ENCOURAGING' — IRAN'S FARS NEWS AGENCY DISMISSES TRUMP'S ANNOUNCEMENT AS 'INCOMPLETE AND INCONSISTENT WITH REALITY,' SAYS HORMUZ WILL 'REMAIN UNDER IRANIAN MANAGEMENT' — CENTCOM MILESTONE: MORE THAN 100 COMMERCIAL VESSELS REDIRECTED SINCE APR 13 (+4 DISABLED, 26 HUMANITARIAN-AID VESSELS PASSED), >15,000 US TROOPS ENFORCING THE BLOCKADE; >1,500 VESSELS / ~22,500 MARINERS TRAPPED — OIL: BRENT ~$105/BBL FRI MAY 22-23 CLOSE ON THE URANIUM ORDER BUT DOWN ~6% ON THE WEEK, ~50% ABOVE PRE-WAR (MARKETS CLOSED SUN MAY 24) — USS BOXER ARG STILL UNDER INDOPACOM; USS GERALD R. FORD HOME AT NORFOLK; LINCOLN + BUSH CSGs IN ARABIAN SEA — NOTE: THE DECISION-WEEKEND BINARY (INTERIM-DEAL SIGNATURE VS 'HIT HARDER') REMAINS AIR/STRIKE-PACKAGE REGISTER, NOT A GROUND-INVASION ORDER (NO GROUND TROOPS STAGED, NO THIRD ARG, NO DRAFT); THE 60-DAY EXTENSION FRAMEWORK IS DEESCALATORY AT THE GROUND RUNG (CONTESTED-UNRESOLVED — open-questions: nyt-intense-preparations-iran-resumption-may-2026)

### Trump: Deal 'Largely Negotiated,' Framework to Be 'Announced Shortly'; Odds a 'Solid 50/50' (May 23, 2026)
- **President Trump announced** that **"an Agreement has been largely negotiated, subject to finalization between the United States of America, the Islamic Republic of Iran, and the various other Countries,"** and that details of a framework — **including the reopening of the Strait of Hormuz** — would be **"announced shortly."**
- In an Axios interview Trump framed the odds as a **"solid 50/50":** **"I think one of two things will happen: either I hit them harder than they have ever been hit, or we are going to sign a deal that is good."** He said he would **decide by Sunday** after a **Saturday meeting with envoys Steve Witkoff and Jared Kushner** (with **VP Vance** expected) plus a **conference call with mediator/Gulf leaders (Egypt, Pakistan, Turkey).** The named alternative to a deal is an **AIR strike** ("hit them harder"), not a ground order. (Axios "Trump says he's 50/50 on Iran deal or bombs," May 23; CNBC; PBS NewsHour; ABC News live updates.)

### 60-Day Ceasefire-Extension Framework — First Phase a 14-Clause MOU, Nuclear File Deferred (May 23, 2026)
- Mediators (**Pakistan + Qatar**, with **Egypt, Saudi Arabia and Turkey**) converged on a **60-day extension** of the ceasefire. Iran's Foreign Ministry spokesman **Esmaeil Baqaei** said the agreement **"will initially take the form of a memorandum of understanding containing 14 clauses,"** with the **nuclear file NOT part of the initial framework** ("we will not discuss the details of the nuclear issue" initially) but addressed **separately over ~30-60 days** of detailed talks.
- **Proposed terms:** a **gradual reopening of the Strait of Hormuz without tolls;** Iran committing to **DISCUSS diluting or transferring its ~440 kg highly-enriched-uranium stockpile;** US **easing of restrictions on Iranian ports;** **limited sanctions relief;** and a **phased release of frozen Iranian assets.** (The Week "US-Iran ceasefire likely to be extended by 60 days," May 23; Ynet "US, Iran near deal, final decision expected within 48 hours," May 23; Jerusalem Post; House of Commons Library CBP-10637.)

### Rubio (May 23): 'Some Progress,' 'We May Have Something to Say'
- **Secretary of State Rubio** said there had been **"some progress"** in the Iran talks and that the US **"may have something to say"** in the coming days, while reiterating that Iran **"can never have a nuclear weapon,"** must **turn over its enriched uranium,** and must **open the strait.** (ABC News live updates; Jerusalem Post live updates; CNBC, May 23.)

### Pakistan Field Marshal Asim Munir Meets Pezeshkian; Talks 'Highly Productive' / 'Encouraging' (May 23, 2026)
- **Pakistan's Field Marshal Asim Munir met Iranian President Masoud Pezeshkian;** Pakistan's military described the **24-hour negotiations as "highly productive"** with **"encouraging"** progress. Pakistani and Qatari negotiators held talks **Thursday and Friday** while staying in **regular contact with US envoy Witkoff.** (The Week, May 23; Ynet; NBC News.)

### Iran Fars: Trump Announcement 'Incomplete and Inconsistent with Reality' (May 23, 2026)
- Iran's **Fars news agency dismissed Trump's "largely negotiated" announcement as "incomplete and inconsistent with reality"** and said the **Strait of Hormuz would "remain under Iranian management";** Iranian officials declined to confirm a deal was imminent — a documented divergence over whether a deal is actually close, drawing on the same record as the optimistic framing. (Fars via wires; CNN/Reuters aggregation, May 23.)

### Oil and Blockade Metrics (May 23-24, 2026)
- **Markets closed Sunday May 24.** **Brent crude climbed back above ~$105/bbl** into the **Friday May 22-23 close** after the Supreme Leader's May 21 order that enriched uranium remain in Iran, but finished **down ~6% on the week** and remains **~50% above pre-war (Feb 28) levels;** WTI near the high-$90s. Trump's "largely negotiated" framing keeps prices in **deal-close range, well below the sustained $130+ that ground-commitment pricing would require.**
- **CENTCOM blockade milestone (May 23):** US forces have now **redirected MORE THAN 100 commercial vessels** since the Apr 13 blockade (up from 85), with **4 disabled** and **26 humanitarian-aid vessels permitted to pass;** **more than 15,000 US troops** are enforcing the blockade. **Project Freedom remains PAUSED;** **>1,500 vessels / ~22,500 mariners remain trapped.** This is **maritime-blockade enforcement, not ground-assault staging.** (US CENTCOM press release "U.S. Blockade of Iran Reaches Milestone of Redirecting 100 Ships"; The Hill; Newsmax; NewsNation; Trading Economics.)

### Carriers / USS Boxer ARG — Ground-Invasion Indicators (May 23-24, 2026)
- **USS Boxer ARG** (LHD-4 + Comstock + Portland + 11th MEU, ~5,000 Marines) **STILL operating under INDOPACOM in the Indian Ocean** per the TWZ carrier tracker; CENTCOM arrival **not yet formally confirmed.**
- **USS Gerald R. Ford (CVN-78) home at Norfolk;** **USS Abraham Lincoln + USS George H.W. Bush CSGs** in the Arabian Sea (**20+ warships** enforcing the blockade; 2 carriers + 1 ARG in theatre).
- **No third ARG; no BCT-scale National Guard activation; no Selective Service/draft/conscription language; Murkowski draft AUMF would BAN ground troops.** The 60-day extension framework involves no ground deployment and is deescalatory at the ground rung; Trump's "hit them harder" alternative keeps the **AIR/STRIKE-PACKAGE backstop** live but adds no ground vector. (TWZ "Where Are The Carriers As Of May 11, 2026"; USNI Fleet & Marine Tracker; Atlantic Council "Tracking US military assets in the Iran war.")

### Russia-Ukraine — POW Swap Continues; Second Round ~June 2 (May 23-24, 2026)
- The **1,000-for-1,000 POW exchange** continues with the **US acting as guarantor**; Ukraine has transferred prisoner lists and is seeking US guarantees the deal is carried out (Zelensky). Putin still refuses to meet Zelensky outside Moscow; a **second round of direct talks is expected ~June 2.** (Kyiv Independent "Ukraine gives Russia POW exchange list, seeks US guarantees"; Critical Threats ISW; Pokrovsk offensive Wikipedia.)

### Latin America — Venezuela / Cuba (May 23-24, 2026)
- **Venezuela:** Maduro and Flores are next due in a New York federal court **June 30, 2026;** acting President Delcy Rodriguez's Caracas-cooperation pattern continues to consolidate. (Wikipedia "Prosecution of Nicolas Maduro and Cilia Flores.")
- **Cuba:** nationwide grid instability and **Havana blackout protests** persist (cacerolazos, burning trash) amid a **>2,000 MW peak deficit;** President Diaz-Canel blames US sanctions. (NPR; CBN; Newsweek; Local10, May 2026.)

### East Asia — North Korea (May 2026)

## 1-PRIOR (MAY 22-23). US-Iran Military Actions — MAY 22-23 (DAY 86): GENUINELY MIXED CYCLE — DIPLOMACY ACCELERATES TOWARD A POSSIBLE INTERIM CEASEFIRE WHILE THE NUCLEAR IMPASSE RE-HARDENS — PAKISTAN FIELD MARSHAL ASIM MUNIR ARRIVES IN TEHRAN (RECEIVED BY INTERIOR MIN ESKANDAR MOMENI); QATARI DELEGATION ALSO ARRIVES; MEDIATORS (PAKISTAN, QATAR, SAUDI, EGYPT, TURKEY) WORK TO FINALIZE A 'LETTER OF INTENT' TO END THE WAR + 30-DAY NEGOTIATION FRAMEWORK — AL-ARABIYA LEAKS AN UNCONFIRMED 9-POINT 'FINAL DRAFT' INTERIM DEAL (IMMEDIATE UNCONDITIONAL CEASEFIRE ALL FRONTS; FREEDOM OF NAVIGATION IN ARABIAN GULF + HORMUZ; JOINT MONITORING; GRADUAL US SANCTIONS LIFTING; OUTSTANDING ISSUES WITHIN 7 DAYS) — NOTABLY EXCLUDES NUCLEAR DISMANTLEMENT / HEU EXPORT / BALLISTIC MISSILES / HEZBOLLAH-HOUTHI SUPPORT — IRAN SUPREME LEADER MOJTABA KHAMENEI ORDERS ENRICHED URANIUM TO REMAIN IN THE COUNTRY (REJECTS TRUMP'S CORE HEU-EXPORT DEMAND); NETANYAHU RIGID — NO END UNTIL HEU FULLY REMOVED — RUBIO (MIAMI): 'GOOD SIGNS' / 'SLIGHT PROGRESS' BUT HORMUZ TOLLING WOULD MAKE A DEAL 'UNFEASIBLE' / 'COMPLETELY ILLEGAL'; 'OTHER OPTIONS' IF TALKS DEAD-END; SYSTEM 'A LITTLE FRACTURED' — US OFFICIAL: NEGOTIATIONS 'AGONIZING,' DRAFTS 'BACK AND FORTH EVERY DAY' — OIL POSTS A WEEKLY LOSS: BRENT +~1% TO ~$103.82/BBL (MAY 22, 9:41 ET) BUT DOWN ~5% ON THE WEEK; HAD JUMPED >2% MAY 21 ON THE SUPREME LEADER URANIUM ORDER — CENTCOM 85 VESSELS DIVERTED + 4 DISABLED (UNCHANGED); >1,500 VESSELS / ~22,500 MARINERS TRAPPED — USS BOXER ARG STILL UNDER INDOPACOM; USS GERALD R. FORD HOME AT NORFOLK — NOTE: A POSSIBLE IMMINENT INTERIM CEASEFIRE THAT PUNTS THE NUCLEAR QUESTION (DEESCALATORY AT THE GROUND RUNG) COLLIDES WITH THE SUPREME LEADER'S URANIUM ORDER + NETANYAHU'S HEU RED LINE + RUBIO'S 'OTHER OPTIONS' (STRIKE BACKSTOP STAYS LIVE); THREATENED OPERATION REMAINS AIR/STRIKE-PACKAGE REGISTER, NOT GROUND-INVASION ORDER (NO GROUND TROOPS STAGED, NO THIRD ARG, NO DRAFT) (CONTESTED-UNRESOLVED — open-questions: nyt-intense-preparations-iran-resumption-may-2026)

### Pakistan Field Marshal Asim Munir Arrives in Tehran; Qatari Delegation Joins; Mediators Work on a 'Letter of Intent' (May 22, 2026)
- **Pakistan's Field Marshal Asim Munir arrived in Tehran** on Friday May 22 to mediate toward a US-Iran deal and was **received by Iranian Interior Minister Eskandar Momeni**; a **Qatari delegation also arrived** to support the negotiations. Munir has been the lead mediator since the war began.
- Mediators aim to finalize a **'letter of intent'** including an agreement to end the war plus principles for **"another 30 days of negotiations on a broader deal that would also address Iran's nuclear program."** **Pakistan, Qatar, Saudi Arabia, Egypt and Turkey** are all involved.
- A **US official** described the negotiations as **"agonizing,"** noting drafts move **"back and forth every day"** without substantial progress; Iran's Foreign Ministry spokesperson said a deal was **not imminent** but talks continue. (Axios "Pakistani field marshal heads to Tehran to try to seal U.S.-Iran deal," May 22; NBC News May 21-22.)

### Al-Arabiya Leaks an Unconfirmed 9-Point 'Final Draft' Interim Deal — Notably Excluding the Nuclear Question (May 22, 2026)
- Saudi network **Al-Arabiya leaked** what it called the **"final draft"** of a US-Iran deal — **UNCONFIRMED** by either government, reportedly possibly announceable **"within hours."** The agreement would activate upon official confirmation from both Washington and Tehran, with outstanding issues tabled for negotiation **within seven days.**
- **Nine points:** (1) an immediate, comprehensive, unconditional ceasefire on all fronts; (2) mutual commitment not to target military, civilian, or economic infrastructure; (3) an end to military operations and a halt to the "media conflict"; (4) respect for territorial integrity and non-interference; (5) **guaranteed freedom of navigation in the Arabian Gulf and the Strait of Hormuz**; (6) a joint mechanism to monitor implementation and resolve disputes; (7) **gradual lifting of US sanctions in exchange for Iran's commitment**; (8) reaffirmed compliance with the UN Charter; (9) outstanding issues discussed later.
- **Major caveat:** the draft **EXCLUDES** the core US/Israeli demands — **dismantling Iran's nuclear programme, exporting its enriched-uranium stockpile, limiting its ballistic-missile capabilities, or ending its support for proxy groups (Hezbollah, Houthis).** (The Week "Final draft of US-Iran deal leaked?...," May 22.)

### Iran Supreme Leader Mojtaba Khamenei Orders Enriched Uranium to Remain in the Country (May 21, 2026)
- Iran's **Supreme Leader Mojtaba Khamenei ordered that the stockpile of enriched uranium "should not leave the country,"** rejecting President Trump's key demand in the talks, per **Reuters citing Iranian sources.**
- Iranian officials argue that **relinquishing control of the material** — the essential component for a nuclear weapon — and sending it overseas would **drastically undermine domestic security** and leave Iran far more vulnerable to future US/Israeli incursions.
- The directive collides with assurances Trump had given Israel that Iran's **highly-enriched-uranium inventory would be completely extracted**, and with **PM Netanyahu's rigid stance that he will not contemplate an end to hostilities until the HEU is entirely removed** from Iranian custody. (Reuters via Business Standard "Khamenei says Iran's nuclear fuel stockpile must remain in country"; Al Arabiya English "Supreme leader says enriched uranium must stay in Iran"; Haaretz, May 21.)

### Rubio (Miami): 'Good Signs' / 'Slight Progress' but a Hormuz Tolling System Would Make a Deal 'Unfeasible'; 'Other Options' if Talks Dead-End (May 21, 2026)
- **Secretary of State Marco Rubio** said there were **"good signs"** / **"some progress"** in talks to end the war, but cautioned **"I don't want to be overly optimistic"** and that Washington is dealing with **"a system that itself is a little fractured."**
- On Hormuz: **"No one is in favor of a tolling system, it can't happen, it would be unacceptable and it would make a diplomatic deal unfeasible"** — calling it **"completely illegal."**
- Rubio said Trump prefers diplomacy to war but there are **"other options"** if talks reach a dead end; reiterated **"Iran can never have a nuclear weapon. It just cannot."** (Times of Israel "Rubio says Iran talks show good signs, but Hormuz tolls would make deal unfeasible"; CBS News live updates; Rappler; Gulf Today; BOE Report, May 21.)

### Oil and Blockade Metrics (May 22, 2026)
- **Oil posted a WEEKLY LOSS** as the US and Iran signaled progress. **Brent crude futures traded ~1% higher at ~$103.82/bbl by 9:41 a.m. ET May 22** but were **down ~5% on the week**; the warring sides remained at loggerheads over Iran's enriched-uranium stockpile and Hormuz tolls.
- Oil had **jumped more than 2% on May 21 after the Supreme Leader's order** that uranium must remain in Iran, before resuming the weekly decline; WTI traded around the high-$90s/low-$100s. Markets are pricing a **deal-close move** well below the **sustained $130+ that ground-commitment pricing would require.**
- The blockade remains effectively in force; **CENTCOM tally still 85 commercial vessels diverted + 4 disabled** (May 18 figure; no fresh count); **>1,500 vessels / ~22,500 mariners remain trapped.** The IRGC's PGSA toll regime remains operational; the leaked draft's "freedom of navigation" clause is the negotiated alternative to the toll regime Rubio called illegal. (CNBC "Oil prices post weekly loss as U.S. and Iran signal progress toward a deal," May 22; CNBC "Oil prices jump more than 2%...," May 21; Trading Economics; CENTCOM running tally.)

### Carriers / USS Boxer ARG — Ground-Invasion Indicators (May 22-23, 2026)
- **USS Boxer ARG** (LHD-4 + Comstock + Portland + 11th MEU, ~5,000 Marines) **STILL operating under INDOPACOM in the Indian Ocean** per the TWZ carrier tracker; CENTCOM arrival could come **"at any time"** but is **not yet formally confirmed.**
- **USS Gerald R. Ford (CVN-78) home at Naval Station Norfolk;** **USS Abraham Lincoln + USS George H.W. Bush CSGs** hold in the Arabian Sea (2 carriers + 1 ARG in theatre).
- **No third ARG; no BCT-scale National Guard activation; no Selective Service/draft/conscription language; Murkowski draft AUMF would BAN ground troops.** The leaked interim ceasefire involves no ground deployment and is deescalatory at the ground rung; the Supreme Leader's uranium order + Netanyahu's HEU red line + Rubio's "other options" keep the AIR/STRIKE-PACKAGE backstop live but add no ground vector. (TWZ carrier tracker; USNI Fleet & Marine Tracker; Atlantic Council "Tracking US military assets in the Iran war.")

### Russia-Ukraine — 253 Frontline Clashes; Pokrovsk Heaviest (52 Assaults Repelled); POW Swap Continues; Second Round ~June 2 (May 22, 2026)
- Ukrainian forces reported **253 combat engagements** in the past 24 hours, with the **Pokrovsk sector heaviest (52 Russian assaults repelled)**; high activity also on the **Huliaipole and Kostiantynivka** directions. Russian total combat losses since Feb 24 2022 reached **~1,353,860** by May 22.
- The **1,000-for-1,000 POW exchange** continues with the **US acting as guarantor**; Putin still refuses to meet Zelensky outside Moscow; a **second round of direct talks is expected ~June 2.** (Ukrinform frontline report May 22; Critical Threats ISW assessments; Pokrovsk offensive Wikipedia.)

### Latin America — Venezuela / Cuba (May 22-23, 2026)
- **Venezuela:** Maduro and Flores are set to return to a New York federal court on **June 30, 2026**; acting President Delcy Rodriguez's Caracas-cooperation pattern (post-Saab-extradition) continues to consolidate, widening the Chavista rift. (Prosecution of Nicolas Maduro and Cilia Flores Wikipedia.)
- **Cuba:** nationwide grid instability persists after the May 14 collapse and fuel exhaustion; AP journalists saw residents in numerous Havana neighborhoods **banging pots and pans and burning trash cans** to protest the blackouts, with the US Embassy reporting protests across Havana met by **"aggressive police repression."** President Diaz-Canel blamed US sanctions and cited an expected peak-demand deficit of **more than 2,000 MW**; only one Russian tanker has reportedly reached the island since January. (NPR "Latest updates on protests in Cuba," May 16; AP via Al Jazeera "Absolutely no fuel...," May 14; Wikipedia 2024-2026 Cuba blackouts.)

### East Asia — North Korea (May 2026)
- **No new ballistic-missile test event May 22-23.** Kim Jong Un continues the **artillery/destroyer buildup** (155mm self-propelled howitzers for the southern border; **Choe Hyon destroyer handover to the navy scheduled mid-June**) and the **"irreversible nuclear"** framing, repeatedly invoking the Iran war to justify the arsenal. (NPR "North Korea says it will deploy new artillery guns," May 8; Bloomberg.)
